{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Gk5qAzix9yR48bHJZ1zgfM","type":"tool_result","content":"\"\"\"The `load` command: validate an ECB history file, store its rates, report the counts.\n\n`parse_history` checks the whole file before the first write, so a rejected file exits 3 and stores\nnothing. Each rate is then written through the declared `rates.replace_rate` operation, which\nreplaces a rate already stored for the same date and currency, so loading a file twice leaves the\ndatabase unchanged.\n\nThe persistence library exposes no transaction operation, so each replace commits on its own. The\nvalidation above is what keeps a rejected file from changing anything; a store failure part way\nthrough a valid file is not rolled back.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\n\nfrom cli.dispatcher import register_command\nfrom fxrates_app.config import open_store\nfrom fxrates_app.ecb import RejectedFile, parse_history\n\nREJECTED = 3\nHELP_FLAG = \"--help\"\n\n\ndef load(rest: list[str]) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Load the file named in `rest[0]` and return the exit code. The argument list is pre-validated.\"\"\"\n    if rest == [HELP_FLAG]:\n        return 0\n    with open(rest[0], encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\") as handle:\n        text = handle.read()\n    try:\n        rates = parse_history(text)\n    except RejectedFile as error:\n        print(f\"error: {error}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return REJECTED\n\n    db = open_store()\n    try:\n        for date, currency, rate in rates:\n            db.rates.replace_rate(date=date, currency=currency, rate=rate)\n    finally:\n        db.close()\n\n    dates = {date for date, _, _ in rates}\n    print(\"dates,rates\")\n    print(f\"{len(dates)},{len(rates)}\")\n    return 0\n\n\nregister_command(\"load\", load)\n----\n\"\"\"Command-line package: `main(argv) -> int` dispatches to handlers registered by command stories.\"\"\"\n\nfrom cli.dispatcher import main, register_command\nfrom cli import init_command  # noqa: F401  registers the `init` handler at import\nfrom cli import load_command  # noqa: F401  registers the `load` handler at import\n\n__all__ = [\"main\", \"register_command\"]\n----\n\"\"\"Command registry and dispatch.\n\nCommand stories register a handler under a command name at import time. `main` hands the\nremaining arguments to the handler and returns the handler's exit code.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\nfrom collections.abc import Callable\n\nfrom cli import usage\n\nUSAGE_ERROR = 2\n\n_handlers: dict[str, Callable[[list[str]], int]] = {}\n\n\ndef register_command(name: str, handler: Callable[[list[str]], int]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Map a command name to its handler; registering a name again replaces the handler.\"\"\"\n    _handlers[name] = handler\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Validate `argv`, dispatch `argv[0]` to its registered handler, and return the exit code.\"\"\"\n    args = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)\n    try:\n        usage.validate(args, _handlers)\n    except usage.UsageError as exc:\n        print(f\"error: {exc}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return USAGE_ERROR\n    handler = _handlers.get(args[0])\n    if handler is None:\n        print(f\"error: command not available in this build: {args[0]}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return USAGE_ERROR\n    return handler(args[1:])\n----\n\"\"\"ECB history file validation: one pure function checks a whole file before any write.\n\n`parse_history` reads the text of an ECB euro reference rate history file and returns every\nnon-N/A value as (date, currency, rate_text). It performs no database access and reads no file.\nRates keep their published text; they are never converted to a number.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport csv\nimport datetime\nimport io\nimport re\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nFIRST_HEADER_FIELD = \"Date\"\nNOT_AVAILABLE = \"N/A\"\n\n_DATE_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}\")\n_CURRENCY_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[A-Z]{3}\")\n_RATE_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?\")\n\n\nclass RejectedFile(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when an ECB history file violates the format; no rate from it may be written.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef parse_history(text: str) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Validate the whole file and return (date, currency, rate_text) for every non-N/A value.\n\n    Raises RejectedFile on the first violation found anywhere in the file.\n    \"\"\"\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(text)))\n    if not rows:\n        raise RejectedFile(\"file has no header\")\n\n    header = _drop_trailing_comma(rows[0])\n    if not header or header[0] != FIRST_HEADER_FIELD:\n        raise RejectedFile(\"header must begin with Date\")\n    currencies = header[1:]\n    for code in currencies:\n        if not _CURRENCY_PATTERN.fullmatch(code):\n            raise RejectedFile(f\"malformed currency code in header: {code!r}\")\n    if len(set(currencies)) != len(currencies):\n        raise RejectedFile(\"currency code repeated in header\")\n\n    rates: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []\n    seen_dates: set[str] = set()\n    for line_number, raw in enumerate(rows[1:], start=2):\n        fields = _drop_trailing_comma(raw)\n        if len(fields) != len(header):\n            raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} has {len(fields)} fields, expected {len(header)}\")\n\n        date_text = fields[0]\n        _check_date(date_text, line_number)\n        if date_text in seen_dates:\n            raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} repeats date {date_text}\")\n        seen_dates.add(date_text)\n\n        for code, value in zip(currencies, fields[1:]):\n            if value == NOT_AVAILABLE:\n                continue\n            _check_rate(value, line_number)\n            rates.append((date_text, code, value))\n\n    return rates\n\n\ndef _drop_trailing_comma(fields: list[str]) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"The ECB writes a trailing comma on each line; the resulting empty last field is ignored.\"\"\"\n    if fields and fields[-1] == \"\":\n        return fields[:-1]\n    return fields\n\n\ndef _check_date(text: str, line_number: int) -> None:\n    if not _DATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(text):\n        raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} has a malformed date: {text!r}\")\n    try:\n        datetime.date.fromisoformat(text)\n    except ValueError as error:\n        raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} has an impossible date: {text!r}\") from error\n\n\ndef _check_rate(text: str, line_number: int) -> None:\n    if not _RATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(text):\n        raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} has a malformed rate: {text!r}\")\n    if Decimal(text) == 0:\n        raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} has a zero rate: {text!r}\")\n----\n\"\"\"FxRates application package.\n\nApplication modules (config, ecb, fetch, rates, history, convert, commands) are added by later\nbuild blocks. Each rate is then written through the declared `rates.replace_rate` operation, which\nreplaces a rate already stored for the same date and currency, so loading a file twice leaves the\ndatabase unchanged.\n\nThe persistence library exposes no transaction operation, so each replace commits on its own. The\nvalidation above is what keeps a rejected file from changing anything; a store failure part way\nthrough a valid file is not rolled back.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\n\nfrom cli.dispatcher import register_command\nfrom fxrates_app.config import open_store\nfrom fxrates_app.ecb import RejectedFile, parse_history\n\nREJECTED = 3\nHELP_FLAG = \"--help\"\n\n\ndef load(rest: list[str]) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Load the file named in `rest[0]` and return the exit code. The argument list is pre-validated.\"\"\"\n    if rest == [HELP_FLAG]:\n        return 0\n    with open(rest[0], encoding=\"utf-8\", newline=\"\") as handle:\n        text = handle.read()\n    try:\n        rates = parse_history(text)\n    except RejectedFile as error:\n        print(f\"error: {error}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return REJECTED\n\n    db = open_store()\n    try:\n        for date, currency, rate in rates:\n            db.rates.replace_rate(date=date, currency=currency, rate=rate)\n    finally:\n        db.close()\n\n    dates = {date for date, _, _ in rates}\n    print(\"dates,rates\")\n    print(f\"{len(dates)},{len(rates)}\")\n    return 0\n\n\nregister_command(\"load\", load)\n----\n\"\"\"Command-line package: `main(argv) -> int` dispatches to handlers registered by command stories.\"\"\"\n\nfrom cli.dispatcher import main, register_command\nfrom cli import init_command  # noqa: F401  registers the `init` handler at import\nfrom cli import load_command  # noqa: F401  registers the `load` handler at import\n\n__all__ = [\"main\", \"register_command\"]\n----\n\"\"\"Command registry and dispatch.\n\nCommand stories register a handler under a command name at import time. `main` hands the\nremaining arguments to the handler and returns the handler's exit code.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sys\nfrom collections.abc import Callable\n\nfrom cli import usage\n\nUSAGE_ERROR = 2\n\n_handlers: dict[str, Callable[[list[str]], int]] = {}\n\n\ndef register_command(name: str, handler: Callable[[list[str]], int]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Map a command name to its handler; registering a name again replaces the handler.\"\"\"\n    _handlers[name] = handler\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Validate `argv`, dispatch `argv[0]` to its registered handler, and return the exit code.\"\"\"\n    args = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)\n    try:\n        usage.validate(args, _handlers)\n    except usage.UsageError as exc:\n        print(f\"error: {exc}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return USAGE_ERROR\n    handler = _handlers.get(args[0])\n    if handler is None:\n        print(f\"error: command not available in this build: {args[0]}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return USAGE_ERROR\n    return handler(args[1:])\n----\n\"\"\"ECB history file validation: one pure function checks a whole file before any write.\n\n`parse_history` reads the text of an ECB euro reference rate history file and returns every\nnon-N/A value as (date, currency, rate_text). It performs no database access and reads no file.\nRates keep their published text; they are never converted to a number.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport csv\nimport datetime\nimport io\nimport re\nfrom decimal import Decimal\n\nFIRST_HEADER_FIELD = \"Date\"\nNOT_AVAILABLE = \"N/A\"\n\n_DATE_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}\")\n_CURRENCY_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[A-Z]{3}\")\n_RATE_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?\")\n\n\nclass RejectedFile(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised when an ECB history file violates the format; no rate from it may be written.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef parse_history(text: str) -> list[tuple[str, str, str]]:\n    \"\"\"Validate the whole file and return (date, currency, rate_text) for every non-N/A value.\n\n    Raises RejectedFile on the first violation found anywhere in the file.\n    \"\"\"\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(text)))\n    if not rows:\n        raise RejectedFile(\"file has no header\")\n\n    header = _drop_trailing_comma(rows[0])\n    if not header or header[0] != FIRST_HEADER_FIELD:\n        raise RejectedFile(\"header must begin with Date\")\n    currencies = header[1:]\n    for code in currencies:\n        if not _CURRENCY_PATTERN.fullmatch(code):\n            raise RejectedFile(f\"malformed currency code in header: {code!r}\")\n    if len(set(currencies)) != len(currencies):\n        raise RejectedFile(\"currency code repeated in header\")\n\n    rates: list[tuple[str, str, str]] = []\n    seen_dates: set[str] = set()\n    for line_number, raw in enumerate(rows[1:], start=2):\n        fields = _drop_trailing_comma(raw)\n        if len(fields) != len(header):\n            raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} has {len(fields)} fields, expected {len(header)}\")\n\n        date_text = fields[0]\n        _check_date(date_text, line_number)\n        if date_text in seen_dates:\n            raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} repeats date {date_text}\")\n        seen_dates.add(date_text)\n\n        for code, value in zip(currencies, fields[1:]):\n            if value == NOT_AVAILABLE:\n                continue\n            _check_rate(value, line_number)\n            rates.append((date_text, code, value))\n\n    return rates\n\n\ndef _drop_trailing_comma(fields: list[str]) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"The ECB writes a trailing comma on each line; the resulting empty last field is ignored.\"\"\"\n    if fields and fields[-1] == \"\":\n        return fields[:-1]\n    return fields\n\n\ndef _check_date(text: str, line_number: int) -> None:\n    if not _DATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(text):\n        raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} has a malformed date: {text!r}\")\n    try:\n        datetime.date.fromisoformat(text)\n    except ValueError as error:\n        raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} has an impossible date: {text!r}\") from error\n\n\ndef _check_rate(text: str, line_number: int) -> None:\n    if not _RATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(text):\n        raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} has a malformed rate: {text!r}\")\n    if Decimal(text) == 0:\n        raise RejectedFile(f\"line {line_number} has a zero rate: {text!r}\")\n----\n\"\"\"FxRates application package.\n\nApplication modules (config, ecb, fetch, rates, history, convert, commands) are added by later\nbuild blocks.
